#### Talk to Nahida

**Nahida:** There you are.

**Nahida:** Miss Nicole truly is an angel. She shows great wisdom in guiding others, and she has already sent word to Natlan on our behalf.

**Nahida:** She said that while the other members of the Hexenzirkel are busy with important work, she is quite free, and she's more than willing to accompany the people of Sumeru for the entire journey.

**Paimon:** That's Nicole, alright! Always super patient and loves to help everyone out. Hehe, she's the best.

**Nahida:** She is. So kind and enthusiastic.

**Nahida:** Well, time for us to get going. Let's head out.

#### Head to the border

This should be one of the closest places to Natlan in all of Sumeru...

There's the barrier Nicole and Nefer mentioned. It completely cuts this nation off from the others...

But why? Why separate you from the rest of the world now, specifically?

And if I'm not mistaken... Irminsul was polluted once before, wasn't it?

(I think that's right? The forbidden knowledge was eliminated, but the fact that Irminsul was polluted should remain unchanged.)

**Mavuika:** Good question. I'm wondering the same thing myself.

That voice... Mavuika! You got here pretty quickly.

**Mavuika:** Hello, Traveler. It's been a while.

**Nahida:** Hello there.

**Mavuika:** Hello, Dendro Archon.

**Nahida:** Mavuika, I apologize for asking you to meet at the border on such short notice.

**Nahida:** I also apologize for not being able to greet you in person. The form in which you see me now is one carried by Traveler. The real me is inside the Mausoleum of King Deshret, and I am reaching out to you with my mind from here.

**Nahida:** Irminsul has been infiltrated by The Doctor. We must be on our guard, as he can spy on information in the world from within the tree. Fortunately, there is no record of Traveler in Irminsul, and you are still within Natlan's borders.

**Mavuika:** My records will be preserved in the Night Kingdom rather than the Ley Lines. As long as the Night Kingdom remains disconnected from the Ley Line cycle, The Doctor has no way of knowing my movements.

**Nahida:** Exactly.

**Mavuika:** So by meeting each other in this way, the meeting essentially never happened.... No wonder Paimon didn't come. You were concerned that her activity would be recorded by Irminsul.

**Mavuika:** The Natlanese have heard about the situation in Sumeru. With the borders sealed off, merchants have been forced to stop their shipments... It's pretty serious, and you're the ones bearing the brunt of it.

**Nahida:** I'd like to address the question you raised at the beginning. I can offer a brief explanation, although it will sound rather cruel.

Hm...

**Nahida:** I don't reach this conclusion gladly, but... Irminsul predates the Heavenly Principles. It existed long ago, when the dragons of Natlan were at the height of their power.

**Nahida:** So even when the people of Sumeru became ill because of pollution, it was not a critical issue from the perspective of Irminsul itself. As long as it continues to function normally, the barrier will not be activated.

**Nahida:** But this time, The Doctor's invasion has directly affected the tree. That is what caused the barrier to deploy.

**Nahida:** The Heavenly Principles established a rule that when Irminsul is plunged into grave danger, a portion of power will be reserved to isolate Sumeru.

**Nahida:** That way, even if Sumeru is destroyed, the outside world at least has some time to catch its breath.

**Mavuika:** We'd certainly need it.

**Nahida:** The Heavenly Principles created almost everything that exists in Teyvat today. To them, preserving the existence of the world and its inhabitants takes priority over protecting Sumeru.

**Mavuika:** Heh. Meaning, protecting Sumeru now falls to you.

**Nahida:** Yes. My own power may be limited, especially compared to yours, but that doesn't mean that we're just gonna sit around and wait for the end. If necessary, I will utilize any and every means at my disposal.

**Mavuika:** Nicole of the Hexenzirkel didn't say exactly what this meeting was about, but after hearing everything you've said so far... I think I can guess where this is going.

That's right. Nahida and I discussed it at length. We both know how presumptuous this is, but...

**Mavuika:** You need the Pyro Gnosis, don't you?

**Nahida:** Yes. You guessed correctly.

**Nahida:** Originally, I only intended to borrow it, but the situation is far graver than I foresaw... So instead, what I ask of you is... to please give the Pyro Gnosis to Sumeru.

**Mavuika:** Hahaha, why do you sound so nervous? There's no need to be shy.

Surely a Gnosis is pretty important to a god?

**Mavuika:** I guess... but it's not as if losing it would suddenly render us powerless.

**Mavuika:** But I'm sure you don't need me to tell you that. With all the nations you've traveled to and all the gods you've met, you must have realized that by now.

**Mavuika:** As far as I'm concerned, a Gnosis is basically an Elemental energy enhancer. But I don't usually use it. You know me... I like to think I'm pretty strong without it.

Yeah... not about to dispute that.

**Mavuika:** Haha. So you see? You don't need to sweat it. Here, take the Pyro Gnosis.

Huh? Just like that?

**Mavuika:** Uh-huh, just like that. To tell the truth, I've always toyed with the idea of giving it to someone who needs it more than I do.

**Mavuika:** I know you accompanied Nahida here to try and increase the chance of success. It's a good thing you did, because your involvement is one of the main reasons you've managed to persuade me.

Are you sure about this? Handing over your Gnosis just because of me...?

**Mavuika:** I won't try to quantify it, but trust me — handing over the Gnosis to Tumaini is far from the craziest idea I can think of.

**Mavuika:** Besides, even if you hadn't come here personally, I think I still would've agreed to Nahida's request.

**Mavuika:** Irminsul doesn't just affect Sumeru. It impacts all of Teyvat. I know all too well how difficult it can be to protect the things you care about... So logically, morally, or however you wanna cut it, helping you is the right thing to do.

**Mavuika:** You don't need to tell me the specifics of your plan. Where the Gnosis is involved, the tighter the secrecy, the higher your chances of success.

**Nahida:** Mavuika...

**Nahida:** My heartfelt thanks go to you and the people of Natlan.

**Mavuika:** Hehe. Don't mention it.

**Mavuika:** Hmm, but now for the hard part — how do I actually give it to you? The Irminsul barrier is right in the way.

I have an idea...

**Nahida:** Just as I thought.

**Mavuika:** Huh, incredible... What did you do?

Just made a lucky guess. This seems to happen quite a lot — things that apply to everyone else don't seem to apply to me. I figured the same might be true here.

**Nahida:** Irminsul usually produces effects based on the records it holds. Since Traveler is a Descender, Irminsul contains no records of him / her, and thus has no way to impede him / her.

**Mavuika:** Haha, is that right... You really are full of surprises.

**Mavuika:** Catch.

Whoa!

Thank you, Mavuika. Thank you for trusting us with something so important.

**Mavuika:** Alright, alright, no need to get so formal. It's not like we're strangers.

**Mavuika:** Besides, there's still one problem... You might be able to pass through the barrier, but I don't know about the Gnosis... Any other surprises for us, Traveler?

Um... Let me try something.

(I have the Pyro Gnosis in my hand... It feels strangely ordinary. Not particularly heavy. And yet, this tiny thing symbolizes the mighty authority of a god...)

(I need to think hard about my memories of Pyro... my memories of journeying through Natlan... All those important experiences I've had.)

...

**Nahida:** The Gnosis disappeared... Traveler, are you alright?

I'm fine.

The Pyro Gnosis disappeared into my body, just like... phlogiston?

**Mavuika:** Hmm... Maybe it resonates with you because you've mastered the power of phlogiston. You might not be recorded in the Ley Lines, but you're still our Tumaini.

...Whew.

(Amazing. I made it back through with the Gnosis.)

**Nahida:** Please allow me to express my gratitude once again, Mavuika.

**Mavuika:** Hehe, pleasure. Well, I've given you all the help I can. The rest is up to you... Good luck.

**Nahida:** We won't let you down.

Yeah. We aren't about to lose!

#### Talk to Nahida

**Nahida:** Traveler, Paimon... how are you feeling?

**Paimon:** Paimon's a teensy bit nervous... but only a teeny-tiny bit. Seems like she's gotten way braver over the last few years, huh?

**Nahida:** Of course. After all the adventures you've had with Traveler, I'm sure you've gained a lot of experience and grown as a person.

I'm doing alright. What about you, Nahida? How are you holding up?

**Nahida:** With the negative effects of Irminsul... I'm a bit like a piece of fruit that's been burrowed into by hungry insects and left to rot. I'd be lying if I said it was nothing.

**Nahida:** But please, don't worry. We all know what we're getting into, and we've prepared as well as we can. I believe in everyone, I believe in you, and I believe in myself.

**Nahida:** Stoking the fire inside oneself is certainly one way to live, but knowing when to rely on one's companions is also a form of great wisdom.

**Nahida:** Hehe. I've been quietly learning little things from everyone, like plucking tufts of fur from a Sumpter Beast.

Me too. With the constant traveling, I'm always learning and growing. That's the journey of life.

**Nahida:** I think there's someone else here who would share that sentiment.

**Wanderer:** Don't worry about what I think. I was just passing by and happened to overhear your conversation.

**Paimon:** What are you talking about, Hat Guy? This is the top floor! No one "passes by" the top floor!

**Wanderer:** Says who? You can fly just like I can. I would have thought it makes sense to you of all people.

...I don't know why, but I feel a little more relaxed now.

**Nahida:** It shows that you feel comfortable laying down your burdens in our presence. It means that Sumeru is a place you can trust, where you know we have your back.

**Nahida:** And we feel exactly the same way around you. That's what it means to be friends.

**Collei:** ...

**Collei:** But it's all about to be over... Tomorrow, it ends for good.

### Truth Amongst the Pages of Purana

"The girl dreamed a very, very long dream. In it, people were holding hands, dancing in a circle, be they sages or fools, dancers or warriors, puppets or statues of gods... It did not matter who they were, or how old, and in that joyous dancing circle was the sum of all wisdom."

**Nahida:** As discussed during our meeting...

**Alhaitham:** A system this powerful is capable of a lot of different things. In the past, the Akasha was primarily used for one-way information transmission, but I suspect it could also serve as a central bridging hub.

**Alhaitham:** In the pursuit of greater knowledge, some scholars have attempted to connect their consciousness to Irminsul through the use of terminals, incense, and meditation.

**Tighnari:** Correct. In academic language, scholars who reach that stage are said to have entered Satyavada Life. Haypasia has performed this practice many times in the past, and she was once under my care for a period of time.

**Alhaitham:** Right. And this at least proves that there is a zone within Irminsul that can be connected to.

**Nahida:** Under normal circumstances, Irminsul would be able to cope with the additional load. But with it already being under strain from other activities, its remaining capacity is limited.

**Nahida:** Thus, it might be possible to shut it down completely by overloading it.

#### Talk to The Doctor

**Fatui Soldier:** We haven't heard a single word from our spy in the desert. I'm afraid he... might have been captured.

**???:** Never mind.

**Fatui Soldier:** But we spent years turning him... And a lot of Mora...

**???:** Nothing comes for free in this world, but the loss is not without its purpose. For a small price, we have wasted a precious amount of the limited time they have. Quite a bargain, if you ask me.

**Fatui Soldier:** ...Understood.

**???:** Weighing profit against loss is a necessary part of life. But to tell the truth, I have been operating at a loss since this all began... since many centuries ago, in fact.

**Regrator:** For instance, why would a banker act as the executor of a will? If you hadn't deposited your personal belongings at Northland Bank, I would have no reason to be here right now.

**Regrator:** Dottore, I've placed your final data backup into the Ley Lines as per our contract. I trust we are now square for the safe deposit fees you paid.

**Il Dottore:** Is it not a little petty to split hairs like this, given our amicable relationship?

**Regrator:** I consider sound bookkeeping a matter of principle.

**Il Dottore:** Do not forget that you only live to see this day thanks to me. In fact, every day of your life since you met me, you've been living on borrowed time.

**Regrator:** Why, that's hardly a fair assessment. I will concede that my survival was perhaps at your whim, but I became a Harbinger on my own merits.

**Regrator:** Managing money is not like other professions. Mine is not a role I could have acquired based on your recommendation alone, and certainly not one I could have lasted so long in without genuine ability.

**Il Dottore:** The information stored in the Ley Lines is quite comprehensive. Rest assured, I have not forgotten how strong your pride is.

**Il Dottore:** Well? I presume you have no sense of fragmentation while talking with me.

**Regrator:** How could anything possibly go wrong with such strict measures in place? The remainder of your soul was split in two, with one half sent to Nod-Krai and the other stored in the most secure vault of the Northland Bank.

**Regrator:** Though I must say, your disdain for the other Segments is quite amusing. Zandik is you, and you are Zandik. Zandik created the Segments — yet you, a Segment yourself, reject him.

**Il Dottore:** "I," that is, Zandik, created Segments of various ages in his later years. To achieve this, he had to freeze time and part with a portion of his soul.

**Il Dottore:** But as constructive as it sounds, it erases my uniqueness. If each Segment is equally me, then by the same token, none of them are truly me.

**Il Dottore:** A soul living forever in the past inevitably holds different views than he did in his later years. The same thing that completes me also restricts me.

**Il Dottore:** You too might ask yourself, which stage of your life you want to be preserved at? The day your business failed? Or the day you were framed by a gang and sold off to a medical lab as a test subject?

**Regrator:** ...We needn't dredge up the past.

**Il Dottore:** This undesirable plan was eventually scrapped. Lesser Lord Kusanali was quite right... I needed an opportunity to eliminate myself.

**Il Dottore:** And after that, I concluded that there was no more effective plan than directly splitting the soul. Once the soul is transferred out, you will find that creating a physical body is far simpler than creating a puppet.

**Regrator:** The other half of your soul suffered a crushing defeat in Nod-Krai, you know.

**Il Dottore:** And yet in the process, I obtained the information I always wanted, did I not? Because now, we finally know where the best testing ground truly lies.

**Regrator:** I once thought your ultimate goal was to ascend to godhood. But as it turns out, that was merely one of your experiments.

**Il Dottore:** Experiments take many forms. Most people are too eager for success, too fixated on the end goal. They forget that the experimental process itself is just as important.

**Il Dottore:** A suspiciously vacant seat, a new face... I trust you can put two and two together? The reason it doesn't exist is because someone tampered with the record.

**Regrator:** What can I say? After all your efforts, you really did uncover the world's secrets. So, is the "new world" you promised a lie?

**Il Dottore:** No, it never was. I am glad to be able to control Irminsul from within... Now, I shall attempt to create a new world inside the existing one.

**Il Dottore:** Almost everyone faces the prospect of death. But not a single person has ever been able to experiment unrestrained in the afterlife through sheer force of will.

**Il Dottore:** I am the only one to have overcome this enormous challenge. Would I not be letting the world down if I didn't make the most of the opportunity to experiment?

**Regrator:** Clearly, the dividing of your soul did not affect the magnitude of your ego. Out of all the versions of you that have ever existed, this "you" is the most insane, and the most radical.

**Il Dottore:** Is that why you chose to work with me? Which was it then... my insanity? Or my radicalism?

**Regrator:** Could I call myself a venture capitalist without a suitably ambitious plan to invest in? Yours happened to fit the bill quite perfectly.

**Il Dottore:** Well then, I must say... while I am surprised enough that you showed up at all, what surprises me more is that you chose to present yourself as my former subordinate and not as the banker, Pantalone.

**Regrator:** Why make things harder than they need to be? Exposing my true identity in a situation like this would only serve to destroy diplomatic relations between Snezhnaya and Sumeru. I daren't imagine the consequences.

**Regrator:** Showing up in the name of the original antagonist gives you the recognition you rightly deserve, while also being sensitive to Snezhnaya's predicament. It was an easy win.

**Regrator:** As for why I'm here... If you're suggesting that your credit is in the negative, I won't argue with that. Many people say that about you.

**Il Dottore:** I'd argue that a significant factor in your willingness to cooperate is the fact that you have but a few years of life left after my death. Living and dying together has a way of binding two parties together rather closely.

**Il Dottore:** Is it not true that a relationship should be judged by both the positive and negative returns it yields?

**Regrator:** Haha, so some of what I said actually made it into your head after all. It's true that I hope you don't die too soon... but don't you find that a strange thing for me to say? After all, you've already had quite a long life.

**Regrator:** ...Especially for a human being.

**Il Dottore:** Let's not pretend that you're any more innocent than I am. Are we not the same in this regard? We both despise the existing laws of this world, and neither of us enjoy being under anyone else's control.

**Regrator:** Is it really so greedy to want the world to operate by a new set of rules?

**Il Dottore:** Of course not, my dear friend. And as I've said before, mutual interest is the foundation for the most reliable of relationships. You can attest to that.

**Regrator:** Hehe. I just hope there never comes a day when I have to work too hard to prove that.

**Regrator:** Many suns and moons passed, seasons changed, and still his confession continued, as if he intended to speak until the end of time...

**Il Dottore:** For someone who despises them so, you never seemed to have any objections to me creating or becoming one.

**Regrator:** Concerned I would terminate our partnership, were you?

**Regrator:** Don't you find people's attitudes toward gods amusing? A god descends from the heavens, and people worship it unreservedly, blindly following whatever rules are given to them.

**Regrator:** And yet, when they see an ordinary person gain godlike levels of power by virtue of their own hard work, they treat him with nothing but suspicion, even if he is a compatriot.

**Il Dottore:** Also, about your recent situation...

**Il Dottore:** Dear me. I must have caused you no end of trouble.

**Il Dottore:** Now I'm curious. How did you explain yourself?

**Il Dottore:** Now then...

**Regrator:** What's on your mind?

**Il Dottore:** The limits of humanity.

**Il Dottore:** It's a pity. Despite running countless calculations, I find there is simply no way to reduce any further the amount of one's soul required to sustain an individual.

**Regrator:** It's rare to hear you admit to being unable to achieve something.

**Il Dottore:** I beg to differ. For instance, as I've told you before, the original "me" was prone to motion sickness and couldn't make long train rides.

**Regrator:** Ah yes, of course. One of the reasons you often sent "yourself" out on errands.

**Il Dottore:** ...

**Regrator:** What are you thinking about now?

**Il Dottore:** A new experiment.

#### Proclaim the inevitability of the action

**Nahida:** This is my decision. Irminsul has been corrupted beyond redemption.

#### Talk to Regrator

**Regrator:** ...What a magnificent fire.

**Regrator:** In Snezhnaya, we light fires to ward off the cold. Fire, it seems, is antithetical to ideas such as cold, frozen, and unchanging.

**The Doctor:** Perhaps it has always been that way. From the beginning, fire has been used to drive away wild beasts and warm the body and mind... You could say it's a form of treatment. One that cures the human heart of mortal fear.

**Regrator:** Mortal fear... Haha, something you haven't been acquainted with in a long time, by the sounds of it.

**The Doctor:** Whereas you, I'm sure, must know the feeling quite well indeed. I remember when we first discussed the elixir of immortality, you claimed that extending your lifespan was about buying more time. But was that really the truth?

**The Doctor:** You fear the prospect of dying a wretched end in this world, don't you?

**Regrator:** Of course I do. I'm only human, after all.

**Regrator:** And while you may have forgotten it, you cannot deny that you, too, were once human, Dottore. I do not believe that there is no such fear in your heart.

**The Doctor:** Perhaps you're right. But I abandoned it long ago and devoted myself to my experiments. I have felt no fear since.

**Regrator:** From the very beginning, I never thought you could win this game.

**The Doctor:** But still you came, contract in hand, to honor our agreement.

**Regrator:** Because I am a reputable partner.

**The Doctor:** And, as we have discussed, experiments are about more than results alone — the process is equally important. I have come here and completed my creation... and I do not regret my decision.

**Regrator:** I'm afraid your journey ends here.

**The Doctor:** After I die, there will be no one to make the elixir of immortality, and your remaining stock will only last a few more years. How then will you prove that you didn't perish in this incident alongside everyone else?

**Regrator:** When I feel optimistic about market environments, I never forcibly adjust them — they have their own direction. And I myself am one such environment.

**Regrator:** I have been quite particular about many things in my life. But where the manner of my death is concerned, I choose not to be.

**The Doctor:** You make yourself sound so magnanimous.

**Regrator:** Oh, but I am, and I hope you will be too. It may have been a long time since anyone told you this, Dottore, but I recognize that you are a truly rare talent.

**The Doctor:** ...Hah.

**The Doctor:** I can see it... The experiment in blasphemy that I've been performing all this time...

**The Doctor:** It has been a long process. And I do not care about the results as much as people might imagine.

**Regrator:** If the very goal of your experiment was to explore infinite possibilities, then there was never any reason for it to end.

**The Doctor:** Indeed. Yet now... the experiment has officially drawn to a close.

**Regrator:** What should I call you? Zandik? Or do you prefer Dottore?

**The Doctor:** Suit yourself. Goodbye, Feofan. This time, that's what it truly is.

**Regrator:** And don't I know it. Goodbye, Zandik.

**The Doctor:** I could have carried out many more experiments... but to me, Lord of Dendro, this outcome is certainly not the worst.

**Nahida:** I believe you. But unfortunately, this is where it all ends for you. This experiment does not belong to you, nor do any of the results.

**The Doctor:** "Experiment"... It sounds like you're trying to outdo me in my own field.

**Nahida:** Wisdom is conveying information in terms that people can clearly understand. You know exactly what I mean.

**The Doctor:** ...Indeed, I can see no further outcomes.

**The Doctor:** Hah... You have a formidable mind, and you are braver than I thought. Setting fire to the very thing your survival depends on... Your previous threat was empty, but this time, it is real.

**Nahida:** When necessary, wisdom can be as fierce as an inferno. Some of the flames that incinerate you come from within yourself.

**The Doctor:** Perhaps you are right. You see what lies at the bottom of the human heart, and that is your wisdom.

**Nahida:** Here is where your journey comes to an end. All that is yet unknown belongs to those of us who will carry on living.

**The Doctor:** ...It seems I have unwittingly been your arch-enemy all along. But now, it's all over.

**Nahida:** I do not deny your intelligence. But what you refer to as "experiments" I would never describe in those terms.

**Nahida:** They are events that take place in the real world, and they are so much more than mere data.

**Nahida:** Goodbye. We will never meet again.

### Your Past Is Like a Garden

The life of sentient beings seems destined to be a treacherous road. Yet, the warm and gentle sunshine still illuminates us who look upon one another. Yet deep and enduring love exists that can help us find ourselves again. And after the crisis passes, the flowers will be there to embrace you.

#### Go to Caravan Ribat

**Dehya:** ...Well, that's the last of them. Everyone on the list is already back where they should be.

**Candace:** It was a long, hard road, but we've finally finished escorting everyone back.

**Candace:** I just pray that we'll never need to leave again. I hope we can stay here forever in this peaceful paradise of our own choosing.

**Sethos:** Paradise... In the annals of the Temple of Silence, that word carries great significance. It represents the hopes and desires of the gods — their longing for a place to rest.

**Dehya:** I wouldn't think gods need such a place.

**Sethos:** Well, that sounds about right to me. Describing them as "friends" sure makes us feel a whole lot closer.

**Sethos:** I will.

**Nahida:** Me... I'm very happy with my current life.

**Nahida:** I do, but projecting a majestic front doesn't really suit me. Besides, my relationship with others isn't simply one of a god and her people.

**Heryshaf:** Not putting on airs might earn you more adoration.

#### Alhaitham and Kaveh Talk

**Kaveh:** Such devastation visited on Sumeru City... So many houses destroyed or damaged in the fighting. Who could have foreseen this...

**Kaveh:** Look at all of this... It's going to take me forever to tidy up! I suppose living below the Akademiya doesn't always work out to your advantage.

**Alhaitham:** I would have thought a little appreciation of my sincerity and effort in helping to clean up this mess might be in order?

**Kaveh:** Helping? Effort!? This is your house — of course you should be putting in the effort! If anything, I'm the one who's helping.

**Alhaitham:** So, you see yourself as just a helper in this situation? I must admit, I'm rather shocked.

**Kaveh:** If you're trying to say that since I live here too, I have an obligation to tidy up... Fine, I suppose I do... Wait a second, stop changing the subject!

**Kaveh:** *sigh* Nevertheless...

**Alhaitham:** In the wake of a great storm, a missing roof is a predictable inconvenience.

**Kaveh:** Can you stop making it seem like I care more about this house than you do? Doesn't it upset you at all to see your home in this state?

**Alhaitham:** I won't pretend it's ideal, but that's no reason to be upset.

**Kaveh:** This is the roof over your head — your shelter from the cold! The furniture is scattered, the paintings have fallen off the walls, the windows are shattered. Don't you feel even a little disheartened?

**Alhaitham:** The way I see it, these problems are easily solved.

**Kaveh:** Ugh, this is precisely why I hate talking to you about sentimental things. It's like banging my head against a wall! *sigh* You simply don't understand just how much work goes into making a proper home...

**Alhaitham:** It sounds like this is causing you a lot of anguish.

**Kaveh:** You mean the house, or the paintings?

**Alhaitham:** I mean your sentimentality.

**Kaveh:** Oh, well, yes... But I'd rather not dwell on such things right now.

**Alhaitham:** Generally speaking, when anguish becomes the norm, people usually make an attempt to improve their situation.

**Kaveh:** ...

**Kaveh:** But there are so many people and things in life that are difficult to change. It's not like you can force them. Besides...

**Kaveh:** ...Anguish can be a source of inspiration, and I'm... not sure I could break my reliance on it...

**Alhaitham:** I'm glad you're able to make yourself think like that.

**Kaveh:** ...What kind of a response is that!? I was stating a profound truth about the very nature of art! Suffering is the cradle of all creation... Even you can't deny that.

**Alhaitham:** And I wasn't trying to. I'm just happy for you, pure and simple. Can't you tell?

**Kaveh:** Happy about what?

**Alhaitham:** Happy that you can always find an answer that backs up your views.

**Kaveh:** You...

**Alhaitham:** More often than not, life is a process of running into problems and then searching for answers. Yours more than most...

**Alhaitham:** Not everyone's so good at justifying things to themselves. It's something that you've been steadily mastering.

**Kaveh:** Hah... What about you, then? What skills have you been "mastering" lately?

**Alhaitham:** Pass me that box.

**Kaveh:** Huh? Okay...

**Alhaitham:** You may consider this my progress on the path to mastery.

**Kaveh:** W—What!?

**Kaveh:** Wait a second... Isn't that box... mine? Hey, it is! It must've fallen off that shelf. Alhaitham, those are my paints and inks!

**Alhaitham:** Would you like me to put it in your bedroom for you?

**Kaveh:** Huh? I mean, sure, if you don't mind...

**Kaveh:** (What kind of "progress" is this? Does he think he can bribe me? Not that giving me back my own things is much of a bribe...)

**Kaveh:** These books on the floor... they're a complete mess. And this one, didn't he say it was an out-of-print collectible? It's completely covered in dust, and some of its pages have even fallen out...

**Kaveh:** *sigh* Guess I should help him repair it. This one too... And this one...

#### Dori and Nefer Talk

**Dori:** Local Sumpter Beasts, imported goods! Such a bright future ahead — I can picture the Mora just rolling in...

**Dori:** Hehe, hahaha... ahahaha!

**Nefer:** You do realize you sound like a choking cow?

**Dori:** It's called being imposing! Can't you feel it? Are you NOT intimidated?

**Nefer:** Oh, I'm positively shaking in my sandals. What commands do you have for me, O mighty Lord Sangemah Bay?

**Dori:** Y—You... Ugh, you've got a lot of nerve, Nefer! You went and told Candace and the others everything, didn't you?

**Dori:** Seriously, do you have any idea how differently they see me now!?

**Nefer:** Surely that's a good thing? I presume they're more disposed to actually like you now, maybe even view you as a friend.

**Dori:** You traded my secret for sympathy! Thanks to you, I've lost every last shred of my mercantile majesty. How am I supposed to make anyone think I'm a big, scary investor now!?

**Nefer:** The dark days of Lord Sangemah Bay are behind you. Why not take this chance to make a few honest friends?

**Dori:** Hmph! Speaking of friends, since when did the notorious Lady Nefer gain such an admiration for holier-than-thou types? A Moonchanter here, an angel there... Hmph, who'd have thought it?

**Nefer:** A coming together of fates, nothing more. No need to throw around accusations of admiration.

**Dori:** Forget it. You're right about one thing, at least — making friends is never a bad thing for a merchant...

**Dori:** Still... *sob*... I really took a loss this time!

**Nefer:** ...

**Dori:** *sob*... Ugh, what are you looking at?

**Nefer:** I'm trying to see if you're really crying or if those are just crocodile tears.

**Nefer:** A little birdy told me that when Lord Sangemah Bay makes money, the tears flow from the center of her eyes, but when she loses it, they flow from the corners. Which is it this time?

**Dori:** Ugh, you're so annoying! I'm not talking to you anymore!

**Katayoun:** Nefer! Thank goodness you're here. I've been looking everywhere for you.

**Dori:** Huh? Well well well, if it isn't the House of Daena's very own librarian! What can I offer you today, Miss Katayoun?

**Katayoun:** Oh, no, I'm not here to buy. I'm here to bring Lady Nefer a book, and a fascinating one at that. We agreed to meet at the entrance to The Mausoleum of King Deshret. One more thing, I bought this copy with my own money — it's not borrowed.

**Nefer:** "Reasoning Knows Not Time of Day, but Suspense Always Follows a Blizzard"... That's quite the mouthful...

**Dori:** Huh! Isn't that... isn't that the one that...

**Katayoun:** It was all the rage for a while. People throughout the city were clamoring to get their hands on a copy. The names inside were all circled — everyone thought it was a riddle.

**Dori:** Don't bring up that book in my presence! I don't even want to hear its name! That thing was nothing but a scam!

**Nefer:** Surprisingly enough, I've actually heard of this book... though it doesn't seem of much consequence. So, what truth lies behind it?

**Katayoun:** You're from the Vahumana, and you're asking ME what the truth is? I'd say the actual "truth" was about the least important part of the whole episode.

**Katayoun:** I brought you this book because it probably has the most interesting backstory of any in the House of Daena's collection, at least in recent years. As for its quality... Well, you can judge that for yourself.

**Dori:** Don't tell me it's actually any good?

**Katayoun:** You may not know this, Lord Sangemah Bay, but Lady Nefer has a fondness for books with, how should I put this... mixed reviews.

**Dori:** No eye for good literature, huh?

**Katayoun:** Perhaps she feels that bad books hide interesting secrets? But I shouldn't put words in her mouth. It's probably fair to say she prefers the conversations that books can spark over the substance of the books themselves...

**Nefer:** Suits my style as an intel broker, wouldn't you say?

**Nefer:** Very interesting, thank you. I'll return it to you once I'm done reading.

**Nefer:** And as it happens, I have something for you, too.

**Katayoun:** ...Incense?

**Nefer:** I imagine you've already used up the incense I gave you last time? Here's some more. This batch is from Nod-Krai, so the scent won't be quite the same.

**Katayoun:** Well, actually... I've been saving the incense you gave me. I couldn't bring myself to waste it without a special occasion.

**Katayoun:** But I'll... I'll gladly accept this, too! And I promise I'll use it this time.

**Nefer:** Katayoun, you haven't changed a bit.

#### Talk to everyone

**Collei:** BOOM!

**Collei:** BOOM!

**Tighnari:** Congratulations, Collei!

**Naphis:** Well done, Collei! After studying for so long, you've finally become a full-fledged ranger!

**Zaha Hadi:** Sure hasn't been easy for you, huh? This really is fantastic news.

**Cyrus:** All grown up! No longer a trainee, but an official Forest Ranger! Hoho, the next step is to secure tenure in the Akademiya!

**Collei:** Thanks, everyone! It's true, I'm an official Forest Ranger now.

**Collei:** Master Tighnari, thank you for your guidance and support. I wish Mr. Cyno were here so I could thank him, too.

**Naphis:** Cyno had a matter to attend to at the Temple of Silence. I don't imagine he'll be back for some time. But don't worry, we can celebrate together once he's finished.

**Tighnari:** I'm afraid the party will have to wait. Collei still has some matters to attend to first.

**Naphis:** Collei? Is... Is everything alright?

**Collei:** No no, it's not what you think! There's nothing wrong with my health — I just have a few things I need to deal with.

**Collei:** I've made plans with Traveler and Paimon to visit Mondstadt. Master Tighnari and Mr. Cyno will be accompanying us, too.

**Naphis:** Well, that's a relief. Let's get together when everyone's back. I'm sure you'll be doing even better by then.

**Collei:** Of course. I have a feeling you're right.

#### Talk to Paimon

*A few days later...*

**Paimon:** Whew! After all that catching up on sleep and food, Paimon's feeling full of vim and vigor again!

For someone who spends so much time resting, your recovery routine is surprisingly simple.

**Paimon:** Hey, Paimon's been working her butt off too, you know! She flew all over the city helping everyone out.

Don't worry, I saw how hard you worked.

**Paimon:** Hehe. Alright, we all know you're the most hard-working person in Teyvat. As long as you're rested and full, that's what really matters.

**Paimon:** Paimon's been looking around the place, and it seems like the city's already on the mend! But Paimon's been wondering how Nahida's doing... We should check in on her!

#### Go to the Sanctuary of Surasthana

**Paimon:** Hey Nahida! And Hat Guy's here, too!

**Nahida:** Traveler, Paimon.

What's going on here?

**Wanderer:** Nothing much. Nahida was just expressing concern for my physical and mental well-being.

**Paimon:** Oh, Traveler told Paimon all about that. We've both been very worried about you... Are you feeling alright?

**Wanderer:** You make it sound like I'm on the brink of some kind of breakdown. Truth is, I'm doing much better than you might imagine.

**Wanderer:** If we can just get to the bottom of Dottore's intentions, we can minimize his negative influence. That's the sort of thing I excel at.

**Nahida:** Just listen to that newfound optimism! It seems like having you enroll in the Vahumana was a truly inspired move. I can really see the positive influence.

**Wanderer:** Observe, a classic Sumeru specialty — chalking everything up to education and learning.

To be honest, even I can sense the change in you.

**Nahida:** Everything Dottore said to you was a way to manipulate your mind. Fortunately, you're no longer under his sway.

**Wanderer:** Can someone as old as me really continue to grow...

**Nahida:** If it helps, just think of yourself as a late bloomer. It's all a matter of phrasing, and it comes down to what standards you measure yourself by.

**Nahida:** For a puppet, developing at such an age is perfectly normal. But if you choose to call yourself a late bloomer, it sounds more like... you consider yourself human.

However you see yourself, well done!

**Wanderer:** Alright, I see what you mean.

Anyway... What's your next step?

**Wanderer:** What else can I do but complete my current courses? Everything else can wait.

**Wanderer:** Anyway, that's enough chit-chat. There are still things in the city I need to help out with. I'll be heading there now.

**Paimon:** Paimon's so happy that Hat Guy is doing better than we thought, hehe.

**Paimon:** But... What about you, Nahida? How are you holding up?

**Nahida:** The Pyro Gnosis burned down the corrupted Irminsul. The flames blazed for seven days and seven nights before they finally subsided. All that was left were the parts underground.

**Nahida:** Now, Aaru shoulders the responsibilities that once belonged to Irminsul. The situation was contained before it got out of hand, so Teyvat's most vital information has largely been preserved.

Irminsul has a strong influence over you, doesn't it? How will you cope now that it's gone?

**Nahida:** There's no need to worry. Thanks to Nicole's extraordinary talents, my connection to Irminsul has been severed and transposed onto Aaru.

**Paimon:** Angels can do things like that!?

**Nahida:** They can. Such abilities are precisely what makes them so powerful. The Goddess of Flowers, Nabu Malikata, once claimed that she, too, could perform feats beyond the reach of mere mortals.

**Nahida:** In my eyes, much of what she went on to achieve reached such transcendent levels. Her and Nicole really are both fascinating and magnificent.

So, does that mean your situation is stable now?

**Nahida:** Correct. Just imagine that Aaru has become the new Irminsul, and the essence of my existence has been bound to it.

**Nahida:** The wisdom of Sumeru has ultimately converged into a single, unified entity. Should we ascribe this all to fate, or is it merely fortune?

**Paimon:** Whatever it is, the important thing is that you're okay, Nahida.

**Naphis:** Dendro Archon! Lesser Lord Kusanali! Oh, thank goodness you're here. Ah, greetings, Traveler and Paimon.

**Nahida:** Sir Naphis, do you have something urgent to say?

**Naphis:** Yes, there's something I need to report. We've received a message from Snezhnaya.

*Naphis hands a letter to Nahida, who reads it...*

**Nahida:** The Fatui Harbinger "The Rooster" sends us his sympathies, along with all the usual pleasantries...

**Nahida:** His stance is that, following the unrest in Nod-Krai, Dottore was subjected to internal censure. This madness was the act of a rogue element, one who cannot be seen to represent the Zapolyarny Palace.

**Naphis:** No less than can be expected...

**Nahida:** Snezhnaya paid a heavy price working to stop him. They even lost one of their own Harbingers.

...

**Nahida:** The letter goes on to say that even so, Snezhnaya cannot claim to be blameless. Apparently, they will deal with Dottore's subordinates in accordance with Snezhnayan law, holding all those involved in his crimes accountable.

**Nahida:** Additionally, they are supplying relief funds and supplies, which are already on route. They hope this will aid in Sumeru's reconstruction.

**Paimon:** Hmph! Sounds like he's got an answer for everything.

**Naphis:** True... But as diplomatic rhetoric goes, it's highly effective. Completely watertight, both in logic and sentiment.

**Nahida:** This disaster has had a profound impact on Sumeru. There is much that I will keep in my heart.

**Nahida:** The matter of Dottore can be seen as settled for now. We are sure to cross paths again in the future, and in that light, Snezhnaya would do well to uphold their responsibilities and keep a tight rein over their actions.

**Nahida:** With Irminsul burned, the flow of fate now carries many more possibilities. I will be watching carefully.

**Naphis:** Perhaps... we can move on to a less serious matter? Dendro Archon, will you eat with us once this matter is dealt with? Everyone wishes to celebrate together. Your presence would be most welcome.

**Nahida:** Of course! Dunyarzad, Cyno, and Haypasia all told me the same thing. If there isn't enough room for everyone, we can arrange back-to-back feasts!

**Naphis:** What a wonderful idea! That just leaves the question of what we should eat...

**Nahida:** Not more tomatoes, surely?

**Naphis:** More? Dendro Archon, what do you mean by "more tomatoes"?

**Nahida:** Those good friends of yours are always talking about tomatoes. Sometimes, when I'm walking through the city, I can even hear them arguing in the fields. Since you see them so often, you must hear about tomatoes even more than I do.

**Nahida:** Tomatoes... Tomatoes... The word has a certain magic to it. Like the autumn fur of a Sumpter Beast, swirling in the wind, sometimes flying up my nose.

**Naphis:** ...Tomatoes... What can we make with tomatoes? Hmm... Wait, why tomatoes?

**Nahida:** Take your time, there's no need to rush. Traveler, Paimon, and I still have somewhere we need to visit. You'll have plenty of time to think it over before I return.

**Naphis:** Does this mean you're going to Natlan? Meeting them there in person... It will require a long journey.

**Nahida:** I know. But this is necessary. I must go myself.

**Nahida:** Traveler, Paimon, we'll set off as soon as you're ready. Is that alright?

Sure! We'll be ready whenever you are.

**Paimon:** You'll hear no objections from Paimon! We should grab some snacks along the way, though! It'll be just like going on a long holiday!

**Nahida:** Wonderful! I'm looking forward to this so much!

#### Go to the Stadium of the Sacred Flame in Natlan

**Nahida:** Natlan is such a lively place!

**Paimon:** It's super cheerful and positive too!

**Nahida:** Mmhmm, I can see that. There are people outside exercising at all hours... Such a healthy way of life!

**Mavuika:** You came? Welcome, welcome! You must be hungry from your journey. Would you like something to eat?

**Paimon:** Thanks, but no thanks! We've been eating the whole way here! We had a Mushroom Platter, Sabz Meat Stew, Butter-Braised Chicken, Padisarah Pudding... Paimon's positively stuffed!

**Mavuika:** Alright, alright! No need for all that detail — I'm starting to get hungry myself...

**Nahida:** Maybe later, then. We can wait till everyone's got an appetite, then all head out for a meal together.

**Mavuika:** Hehe, sounds like a plan. Let's find somewhere to sit down.

#### Talk to Mavuika

**Mavuika:** I should tell you, I already have some sense of why you've come.

**Nahida:** Why's that, Mavuika?

**Mavuika:** To say thank you.

Your senses are spot on. But on top of that, we also feel guilty.

**Mavuika:** Because the Pyro Gnosis has vanished? Mm, I sensed its loss.

**Nahida:** Despite scouring through the remnants of Irminsul, we couldn't find any sign of the Pyro Gnosis... not even any trace of it burning to ash.

**Mavuika:** That's certainly unusual. Still, the potential fallout from Irminsul's loss is significant, to say the least. Perhaps the Pyro Gnosis's disappearance is simply a knock-on effect of the impact it's had on a certain strand of fate.

**Mavuika:** There's no need to feel guilty. We were clear that this time, there was to be no "returning" — only "giving," pure and simple.

**Nahida:** "Thank you" really is a fascinating phrase. We use it to express gratitude for the smallest of things, yet when faced with a kindness too immense to describe, we use exactly the same words.

**Nahida:** It's not that we lack other means of expression — it's that when we truly need to express our feelings, the simplest and purest words are always the most powerful.

**Mavuika:** You're right. In Natlan, we prefer simple expressions too. "Companionship," "friendship," "support"... they might not sound fancy, but the sentiment behind them is sincere.

**Nahida:** That's wonderful to hear! Sincere feelings are often a vehicle for the deepest wisdom of all.

**Mavuika:** Irminsul's destruction doesn't seem to have done you much harm. I'm glad to see you're doing well.

**Nahida:** There's still a lot of work to do, but before we begin, we felt it was important to come here and speak with you.

Partly to express our gratitude, but also because we felt... that seeing you, in a sense, is like basking in warm sunlight.

**Mavuika:** Haha, that's a nice way of putting it. I like that.

**Mavuika:** To tell you the truth, though, I'm not a fan of somber meetings. So, since there's nothing more to say, why don't we go bask in some actual sunlight? The weather's beautiful today.

**Paimon:** What a great idea! Paimon's gonna lie back and soak up some rays!

**Nahida:** Hah...

In that case, I think I'll lie down for a bit too before we head to our next stop.

**Mavuika:** And where might that be?

Mondstadt.

Paimon and I are gonna head back there with Collei and the gang.

**Nahida:** Long ago, I sensed that the god remains inside Collei had come from the Goddess of Flowers, but I kept it a secret.

**Nahida:** Those remains weren't hurting Collei, not as she is now — they simply exist. Besides, I could sense a deep yearning within her.

**Nahida:** A yearning... for an ordinary life. A yearning to hold onto the hard-won happiness she now enjoys. With someone like her, I have a responsibility to keep her secret.

She should be ready to face the truth by now.

**Mavuika:** Whenever people meet, there is always the potential to create warmth and kindness. Every child grows up, but even as adults, they are still children in the eyes of certain people.

**Paimon:** Oh! Just like how Paimon sometimes sees Nahida as a little kid!

**Nahida:** Is that so? Well, anyone who's friends with a kid is probably a kid themselves.

**Paimon:** Of course, and there's nothing wrong with that! When we get back, we'll actually be hanging out with another of our friends who happens to be a kid — or, more like looking after, in her case. Anyway, we'll always be there to watch out for our friends!

**Collei:** Dear Sucrose, when you receive this letter, I'll probably be as busy as always... but busy with wonderful things. That's because I'm no longer a trainee — I'm now an official Forest Ranger!

**Collei:** Impressive, right? Cyno said that official rangers sound much tougher than trainees. Though, he did say I was already pretty tough to start out with.

**Collei:** You probably heard that something big happened in Sumeru recently. Don't worry, it's all been taken care of. We're heading to Mondstadt soon, so if you're free, maybe we could meet up?

**Collei:** Plus, it's not just me — a few of us are going. Hey, do you still remember that time we all got together? You know, when we all went to the Windblume Festival. It'll be just like that!

#### Talk to everyone

*Two days later, by the gates of Mondstadt...*

**Cyno:** Tighnari, is that...

**Tighnari:** Don't you say it! Is that the Great "Vay-iew-vyastra"? Behold the Great Vayuvyastra, General Mahamatra.

**Cyno:** How times have changed. I wasn't expecting you to beat me to the punchline... You've changed!

**Collei:** You've really upped the aggression in your battle against Cyno's jokes!

**Cyno:** Collei! How did you know what I was going to say!?

**Tighnari:** For the love of...

**Paimon:** Hey—!

**Collei:** Traveler, Paimon, you're right on time.

I hope we haven't kept you waiting?

**Sucrose:** Collei!

**Collei:** Sucrose! It's been so long! How have you been?

**Sucrose:** I've been well! Everything's going great. What about you? Are you still busy?

**Collei:** Not at the moment. Oh, right, that juicer you gave us works amazing! Faruzan loves it!

**Sucrose:** Seriously? That's wonderful! I don't suppose you drew up a detailed test report? Oh! What fruits did you use? How was the texture of the final product?

**Albedo:** Sucrose, you might want to try asking your questions one at a time...

**Paimon:** Albedo! Paimon didn't see you there!

**Albedo:** It's been a while since all of us were together like this. How about we follow the same plan as last time?

**Cyno:** Good idea, my friend, you never disappoint.

**Albedo:** So, what are your "identities" this time?

**Tighnari:** Don't remind him...

**Cyno:** Ahem... First things first, allow me to introduce our very own "official" Forest Ranger Collei. The rest of us are simply her humble travel companions.

**Tighnari:** This sounds like one of those mushy stories that gets people hooked on some corny, heart-warming premise.

**Cyno:** Oh, is our story not heart-warming? If I recall correctly, there was even a scene like this in King of Invokations.

**Collei:** Besides being heartwarming, our story's also about tough, impressive-sounding job titles! Uhm... At least... that's what I think.

#### Go to the Mondstadt Library

*You, Collei, and the others meet with the Knights of Favonius. After exchanging warm greetings, you head to the library, where you run into...*

**Nicole:** Oh! You're here already? I'm still giving Dodoco a little trim. I won't be a moment.

**Paimon:** A trim? Does Dodoco need haircuts, too?

**Nicole:** Of course! It's important to know how to take loving care of the apples of your eye!

**Nicole:** Hehe. All of these trivial matters get left to me. It's because I'm an angel, and angels are, in a way, the guardians of all Teyvat.

**Paimon:** Ohhh, right. Well, that's definitely an impressive-sounding job title!

**Tighnari:** What are you writing?

**Cyno:** I'm just brainstorming some cool-sounding lines.

**Albedo:** Oh! Write them on these card backs.

**Cyno:** Of course!

**Nicole:** Ahem! Clearly, my lines are pretty amazing, wouldn't you say?

**Collei:** Miss Nicole...

**Nicole:** Collei, you seem a little nervous.

**Collei:** I was just wondering... who was the person you mentioned who needed their wish fulfilled?

**Tighnari:** ...Wish? What do you mean?

**Collei:** Miss Nicole asked if we could meet up in Mondstadt once everything was over. She said a friend of hers had a wish that needed my help to come true.

**Cyno:** Well, you never mentioned that before. All we knew was that you'd arranged to meet in Mondstadt. But we thought it had something to do with the god remains.

**Nicole:** Hmm... To be fair, everything I told you was true. Anyway, my friend is waiting.

**Nicole:** Come on, let's pay her a visit.

#### Go beneath the great tree at Windrise

**Collei:** Huh, you're...

**Liloupar:** Surprised? I bet you weren't expecting to meet a woman like me beneath this beautiful tree in a faraway land.

**Liloupar:** "An oasis will spring from the place where a Jinn resides..." That saying falls somewhat flat here in Mondstadt, given that everywhere is already so lush and green.

**Paimon:** Liloupar!? What are you doing here?

**Liloupar:** I made friends with Miss Nicole. I asked her to bring me here and then invite Collei to come.

**Liloupar:** The prologue to this story is that I have a wish. It has stirred within my heart for centuries, millennia even. Originally, it was merely Al-Ahmar's lie, but now it has become reality.

Could this be...

**Collei:** ...related to the Goddess of Flowers?

**Liloupar:** My dear young lady... My mistress left her final trace in this world upon you. I can't tell you how glad I am to see you here.

**Liloupar:** And now I have seen you... and peered into your heart. You have experienced hardships and suffered setbacks. The torment an ordinary person might expect to face has befallen you threefold. This is the source of your resilience.

**Liloupar:** But, beneath all this, I've witnessed the seeds of desire. You yearn to return to the simple life you once knew, do you not?

**Collei:** ...I do. I want to throw away everything that isn't mine.

**Liloupar:** But how do you decide what is yours? The scars that others carve into your flesh, the kindness and love they inscribe, are these things not yours as well?

**Collei:** They are. This is something I've talked about with Miss Nicole.

**Collei:** Miss Nicole said I should learn how to adjust my standards. I just want to live as an ordinary human. Anything beyond that isn't mine.

**Liloupar:** Hehe... Don't you want anything from the remains of the Goddess of Flowers?

**Collei:** She gave me a revelation once. I think that's already more than enough.

**Liloupar:** In the past, you would not have possessed such a rich heart. Your brief life has taught you to hold on to everything good, to home in on kindness...

**Liloupar:** How fascinating. You're nothing like Nabu Malikata, and you're certainly not her.

**Collei:** O—Of course not... I'm not some great and powerful being, Miss Liloupar.

**Liloupar:** Yet I am a Jinn. We exist because of the Lord of Flowers. We revere and love her. My wish is to see her one last time, to give everything for her.

**Liloupar:** Collei, can you do this for me?

**Collei:** But... Her remains won't be easy to remove. Are you sure this is what you want?

**Liloupar:** That's beside the point. First, I need to know, are you willing to do something for the soul of this lonely, wandering woman?

**Collei:** I... I'm willing.

**Liloupar:** But look, you know nothing about me. Even if you have heard the stories about me, all you would know is that I was rightly punished for the many evil deeds I committed.

**Liloupar:** So you do know. Your lips are quivering, your eyes trembling... and still you're willing to do something for me. Life has been cruel to you, and yet you've learned to be kind.

**Liloupar:** My dear young lady, to repay your kindness, I too must play my part.

**Nicole:** Are you sure about this, Liloupar?

**Liloupar:** I yearn to see my mistress. For this moment, the world can burn to ashes, the universe be engulfed in flame... I care about nothing else.

**Liloupar:** And you, Collei, I can give you a gift. You'll become just another ordinary human.

**Collei:** But Miss Liloupar, what will happen to you?

**Liloupar:** My wish will be fulfilled, and I can die happy.

**Collei:** Why would you wish for that!?

**Liloupar:** A long, unending life might seem desirable for one who has never possessed it, yet it brings torment to one who has. My remains are scattered across the yellow sands, but my soul can find no peace.

**Liloupar:** Since you've promised to grant my wish, give me that release. Allow the dying to die, and let the dead rest in peace. You have my thanks.

**Nicole:** Collei, there's no pressure on you. What she said... It's all true. A wish is a genuine desire, after all.

**Collei:** It's as if you want to use the last of your life to give me a better future...

**Liloupar:** Nothing so selfless. We'll be using our futures to fulfill each other's wishes. This is a noble act, a beautiful and secret transaction between two women.

**Collei:** ...

**Collei:** I understand... Miss Liloupar, I'm ready.

**Liloupar:** Close your eyes, Collei. Then open your soul. From there, I will embrace my mistress.

**Liloupar:** The downpour of flowers is your touch. Like returning to the very beginning... Like surrendering to your grace. My mistress.

**Liloupar:** People say that devotion is beautiful, yet filled with pain. How foolish of them. For me, devotion is beautiful, pure, and painless. Devotion is my love.

**Liloupar:** ...Thank you.

**Paimon:** Liloupar... She disappeared?

*The Jinn's magic bottle falls silently to the ground, now completely empty.*

She left, carrying the blessing of flowers.

**Nicole:** Collei, are you alright?

**Collei:** ...I'm fine. Now that the remains are gone, I...

**Collei:** ...Don't actually feel any different at all.

...

**Nahida:** Those remains weren't hurting Collei, not as she is now — they simply exist. Besides, I could sense a deep yearning within her.

(Collei must have already received what she was yearning for.)

**Collei:** I'm doing fine. I feel relaxed. There's really no need to worry.

**Nicole:** Then my wish has come true, too.

**Collei:** To become ordinary?

**Nicole:** Hehe, that's just one of my wishes. Actually, I have a few more. For instance, I want to protect the people I meet and help them find happiness... that sort of thing.

**Collei:** So between Miss Liloupar and me, you've fulfilled two wishes already, and protected two people.

**Nicole:** Wonderful, isn't it? I feel so fantastically happy!

**Collei:** I guess that settles things here.

**Cyno:** So, what's next? What do you want to do when we get back to town?

**Collei:** I...

**Sucrose:** Collei, look who I've brought to see you!

**Amber:** Hey, Collei! I heard you guys were holding some kind of secret event. How come you started without me?

**Collei:** Huh? Oh, no, it's nothing like that! We were... just saying goodbye to an old friend.

**Amber:** Really? Who?

**Collei:** Me. The old me.

**Amber:** So that's what this is all about... Did you come all the way to Mondstadt for that? Because we knew the old you, so we could witness the change...

**Collei:** Exactly. The old me is gone. I puffed her into the air, and she became a fluffy pink cloud, floating into the sky, before disappearing forever.

**Sucrose:** Do you think... maybe up in the sky, she has friends waiting for her with test tubes, flasks, and bows?

**Paimon:** Oh... When you put it like that, it doesn't sound so bad after all!

**Collei:** What do you think, Miss Nicole?

**Nicole:** I think, maybe that's right...

**Nicole:** At least... if it were up to me, I'd consider that a happy ending.

**Collei:** Dear Sucrose, even though my childhood ended long ago, these past few days have really felt like growing up again. I've become stronger, and I feel like I can finally accomplish things by myself.

**Collei:** Surely someone as mysterious as you will find a friend like this fascinating? Always growing, always thriving like a plant.

**Collei:** I came to say goodbye to my old self. I know that after this, she'll be gone. Thinking about it makes me feel a little lonely... I know the weakness that kept me company will one day leave me too.

**Collei:** But somewhere in my heart, I feel a great sense of joy. It feels like I've always wanted to take this step — to walk towards the new me.

**尼可:** 指引人类将他们心中的奇迹付诸现实，就是我的奇迹。

#### 前往国境线

这里应该就是须弥最接近纳塔的地方之一了…

那个就是尼可和奈芙尔说的屏障。完全阻隔在须弥与其他国家之间…

可是，为什么呢？为什么非要在这种时候把你们和其他地方隔开？

而且，我记得…世界树曾经被污染过，对吧？

**玛薇卡:** 你好，草神纳西妲。

**纳西妲:** 也很抱歉以这样的形态与你对话。你现在见到的我借由旅行者的身体存在，我正从赤王陵内与你意念触碰。

**纳西妲:** 世界树遭到了「博士」入侵，必须提防他从树的内部窥探世间信息。而旅行者不被世界树记录，你也还在纳塔境内。

**玛薇卡:** ——我的记录会被留存在夜神之国而非地脉，只要夜神之国不接入地脉循环，「博士」就无法知晓我的行动。

**纳西妲:** 没错。

**玛薇卡:** 我们三个用这种方式相见，基本就等同于不存在。难怪派蒙不在，你们也是考虑到了她会被世界树记录这一点吧。

**玛薇卡:** 须弥的情况，我们纳塔人都有所耳闻。边界被封锁，商队被迫中止运输…情况相当严重，你们辛苦了。

**纳西妲:** 关于你们刚刚的问题，我要稍作说明，不过接下来的话稍微有些残酷。

没错。

**纳西妲:** 我并不想这样定论…但世界树先于天理存在，早在纳塔龙族鼎盛的时期，它就存在。

**纳西妲:** 哪怕须弥的子民因此出现什么病症，对世界树而言都不是十分严重的问题。它本身仍在正常运转，屏障自然不会启动。

**纳西妲:** 而现在，「博士」的入侵切实影响到世界树本身，屏障便展开了。

**纳西妲:** 天理定下这一规则：当世界树陷入严重危机时，会确保留出一部分力量将须弥隔绝。

**纳西妲:** 这样即便须弥毁灭，外界也有喘息的时间。

**玛薇卡:** 这一点我们也深有共鸣。

**纳西妲:** 天理创造了提瓦特如今的绝大部分事物。对天理而言，维护大部分事物存在下去的优先级，高于保护须弥。

**玛薇卡:** 所以你要用自己的方式保护须弥？

**纳西妲:** 是的。相比你，我的个体力量要弱一些，但这不意味着须弥要坐以待毙。必要时，我将动用一切手段。

**玛薇卡:** 魔女会的尼可小姐没明说，不过听完这些，我多少也猜到你们的来意了。

是的，我和纳西妲认真讨论过，我们都知道这有些僭越，但…

**玛薇卡:** ——你们需要火神之心，对吧？

**纳西妲:** 嗯，你猜得很准。

**纳西妲:** 我原本想说「借用」这个词，可事态远超预期，所以…我请求你，将火神之心给予须弥。

**玛薇卡:** 哈哈哈，为什么犹豫？你不用不好意思。

神之心对一个神来说，很重要吧？

**玛薇卡:** 不能说不重要，但也没到拿走就会让我们变得无力的地步。

**玛薇卡:** 你们去过那么多国家，与许多神明打过交道，应该知道这点吧。

**玛薇卡:** 于我而言，神之心相当于元素增幅器。不过我平时用不到它，就像你们知道的那样…我觉得我还挺强的。

我们都知道。

**玛薇卡:** 哈哈哈，所以我才说，你们应该放轻松些。来，这是火神之心。

欸？就这样给我吗？

**玛薇卡:** 拿出来又没难度。而且，我原本也想过将它交给有需要的人。

**玛薇卡:** 旅行者，你陪纳西妲来找我，是想增加这件事的成功率。而实际上，你的出现恰恰也是说服我的理由之一。

真的可以吗？事关极度重要的神之心…

**玛薇卡:** 我不会量化你的付出，可你要相信，将神之心交给杜麦尼对我来说不是什么很离奇的事。

**玛薇卡:** 而且，哪怕旅行者不来，纳西妲，我也会同意你的请求。

**玛薇卡:** 世界树不只影响须弥，它能作用于全提瓦特。我非常清楚「守护」有多难，于情于理都该尽力帮你。

**玛薇卡:** 具体计划不用告诉我。神之心的事，越是保密，成功率就越高。

**纳西妲:** 玛薇卡…

**纳西妲:** 衷心感谢你和纳塔的人民。

**玛薇卡:** 别客气。

**玛薇卡:** 唔，不过目前的难点是怎么将东西递给你们。世界树制造的屏障还挡在中间呢。

我有个想法。

**纳西妲:** 果然。

**玛薇卡:** 好神奇啊，旅行者，你做了什么？

我也是瞎猜的，不是经常有那种事吗？大家是一个状态，我是另一个状态。这次说不定也一样。

**纳西妲:** 世界树通常基于记录产生影响。旅行者是降临者，世界树内没有关于他 / 她的记录，也就无法阻挡他 / 她。

**玛薇卡:** 原来是这样，你还真有意思啊。

**玛薇卡:** 接好。

哇！

**玛薇卡:** 行了行了，不用谢我，我们的交情你心里有数。

**玛薇卡:** 而且，还有个问题。你能穿过屏障，神之心可未必…旅行者，有办法吗？

我…可以试试。

（好好想一想，火的回忆…在纳塔旅行的记忆…那些都是非常非常重要的经历。）

……

**纳西妲:** 神之心消失了…旅行者，你怎么样？

我没事。

火神之心…像燃素一样消失在我身体里了？

**玛薇卡:** 说不定就是因为你掌握了燃素，它能与你共鸣。就算不被地脉记录，你到底还是我们的杜麦尼啊。

…呼。

（真的过来了，好神奇啊。）

**玛薇卡:** 我能帮的忙就到这里。后面交给你们了，加油。

**纳西妲:** 而那恰恰也是我们对你的感受，朋友的意义正在于此。

**「博士」:** 说到恐惧，我很难不想起你。最初我们谈论长生不老药，你说延长寿命是为了争取更多时间，可实际上呢？

**「博士」:** 你害怕狼狈地离开这个世界吗？

**「富人」:** 我是人，总会害怕狼狈的。

**「富人」:** 因为我是有信誉的合作者。

**「博士」:** 而正如我们所说的，实验并不只有结果，过程也很重要。我来到这里，完成了创想…没什么不好。

**「博士」:** 我死后，没人能做长生不老药，你的库存也只够再支撑几年而已。那你如何证明自己不是在这起事件中陪葬了？

**「富人」:** 我从不强硬地调整一些我看好的市场环境，因为它自有方向。而我自己亦是这样一个环境。

**「富人」:** 刻意的部分足够多了，至少在死亡的方式上，我选择不那么刻意。

**「博士」:** 说得好像你是一个真正豁达的人。

**「富人」:** 我不仅是，也希望你是。多托雷，也许很久没人说过，但我认可你是不可多得的奇才。

**「博士」:** …哈。

**「博士」:** 我能看到，我一直在进行的那场，关于亵渎的实验…

**「博士」:** 它很漫长，而我没有人们想象中那么看重结果。

**「博士」:** 的确。现在…实验正式告结。

**「富人」:** 该称呼你什么呢，赞迪克？或者你更喜欢「多托雷」？

**「博士」:** 随你。费奥潘，再见。这是最后一次。

**「富人」:** 我怎么会不知道呢。再见，赞迪克。

**「博士」:** 我本可以完成更多实验……不过草之主，现在的结果于我而言，也并不糟糕。

**纳西妲:** 我相信你说的话，但很遗憾，你的一切都结束了。这场实验不属于你，一切实验的成果也都不属于你。

**「博士」:** 「实验」。听起来像是你要在我的领域胜我一筹。

**纳西妲:** 智慧是以人们最能理解的方式传送信息。你能读懂我的意思。

**「博士」:** …我确实无法再看到更多结果了。

**「博士」:** 哈…你有十足的头脑，也比我想的更勇敢。竟敢放火烧掉自己赖以为生的事物…上一次你的威胁是假，这一次却是真的。

**纳西妲:** 智慧在必要时，也可暴烈如火焰。烧掉你的火焰里，也包含你自己。

**纳西妲:** 你终究停止在这里。未知的一切，都属于我们这些依旧存在的生命。

**纳西妲:** 那是真真切切发生在世间的事，比数据更有意义。

**纳西妲:** 再见。我们不会有下一次会面了。

### 你的往事如一座花园

智慧生命的一生似乎注定要是坎坷之途。好在还有温暖而柔和的阳光，照亮你我望见彼此，好在还有厚重又绵长的爱，帮助人们寻回自己。苦难后，总有鲜花环抱而来。

**利露帕尔:** 若在从前，你不会拥有如此富足的心。短暂的生活教会你捕获好的事物，看到善良…

**利露帕尔:** 啊，真有意思。你和娜布·玛莉卡塔截然不同，你不是我的女主人。

**柯莱:** 这、这当然…我绝对不是那么伟大的存在，利露帕尔小姐。

**利露帕尔:** 而我是镇灵，我们因花之女主人存在，敬她，爱她。我的心愿是再见她一面，并为她付出一切。

**利露帕尔:** 柯莱，你能为我做到吗？

**柯莱:** 可是…这个残渣并不是那么轻易就能取出来的东西。你确定吗？

**利露帕尔:** 那是其次了。请先回答，你愿意为我这孤苦的流浪女人的灵魂做些什么吗？

**柯莱:** 我…我愿意。

**利露帕尔:** 可你看，其实你根本不认得我，即便翻阅故事，也只知道我这传说中的恶人因曾犯下种种恶行而遭受了应得的惩戒。

**利露帕尔:** 你知道的。你的嘴唇抖动，眼皮震颤…即便如此，也愿意为我做些什么。生活恶毒地待你，你依然习得善良。

**利露帕尔:** 亲爱的姑娘，为报答你的善良，我也要行动起来。

**尼可:** 利露帕尔，你真的想好了？

**利露帕尔:** 我想再见女主人一面。为了这一瞬间，世界都可以焚毁，宇宙可以燃烧…我不在乎任何。

**利露帕尔:** 而你，柯莱，我会送你一样小礼物。你将成为平凡之人。

**柯莱:** 利露帕尔小姐，那你会怎么样？

**利露帕尔:** 我的心愿会得到满足，我会幸福地死去。

**柯莱:** 怎么能这样呢？！

**利露帕尔:** 漫长无尽头的生命，未曾拥有者艳羡，已然得到者痛苦。我的残骸落在黄沙各处，我的灵魂难以安息。

**利露帕尔:** 你既然答应实现我的愿望，便把那些给我吧。令将死者死，令亡者安息。我感谢你。

**尼可:** 柯莱，不要有压力，她说的…都是实话。愿望本也是真实的渴求。

**柯莱:** 这就好像您用最后的生命，为我留下了好的未来一样…

**利露帕尔:** 不，我们不过是用彼此的未来达成了对方的心愿。这是崇高的事，女人之间美丽而隐秘的交易。

**柯莱:** ……

**柯莱:** 我明白了。那…利露帕尔小姐，有劳你。

**利露帕尔:** 闭上眼睛，柯莱。然后张开你的灵魂，我要从那里拥抱我的主人。

**利露帕尔:** 花的暴雨便是您的触碰。如同回到最初…如同臣服在您的恩典之中，我的主人。

**利露帕尔:** 人们总说忠诚是美丽而痛苦的事，那很愚昧。于我，忠诚摒弃了痛苦，美丽而纯粹。忠诚便是我的爱。

**利露帕尔:** …谢谢。

**派蒙:** 利露帕尔…消失了？

*魔瓶静静地落到地上，内里已没有了镇灵的气息。*

她离开了，带着花的祝福。

**尼可:** 柯莱，你还好吗？

**柯莱:** …我很好。而且…取掉这些残渣以后，我发现…

**柯莱:** 我发现，自己和以前并没有什么区别。

……

**纳西妲:** 首先，那些残渣对如今的柯莱没有负面作用，它们只是普通地存在着。其次，我从她身上感受到了渴望。

（柯莱所渴望的东西，已经降临在她身上了啊。）

**柯莱:** 我现在很好，很轻松。请不要担心。

**尼可:** 那我的愿望也实现了呢。

**柯莱:** 变得平凡吗？

**尼可:** 啊，那只是我的一个愿望啦，其实还有一些，比如说，我要守护遇见的人，让他们获得幸福…之类的。

**柯莱:** 那我和利露帕尔小姐加起来，已经实现了两个愿望。你守护了两个人。

**尼可:** 是啊，我也觉得很高兴呢。

**柯莱:** 这里的事，就算解决了。

**赛诺:** 接下来怎么说？回城里想先做什么？

**柯莱:** 我想…

**砂糖:** 柯莱，我带安柏来啦！

**安柏:** 喂，柯莱！听说你们在搞什么秘密活动，怎么不带我就开始了？

**柯莱:** 欸？啊，不是啦！在…在给一个朋友送行而已。

**安柏:** 真的吗？谁呢？

**柯莱:** 从前的我啊。

**安柏:** 居然是这样…所以才要回到蒙德来？因为我们认识从前的你，可以为你见证这些。

**柯莱:** 是的。从前的我，已经离开了。我往天上吹了气，她变成粉色的云，悄悄飞起来，消失啦。

**砂糖:** 那…也许天上会有拿着试管和烧瓶，拿着弓箭的朋友在那里等她？

**派蒙:** 要是那样，也不错呢！

**柯莱:** 尼可小姐觉得呢？

**尼可:** 唔，会的吧？

**尼可:** 至少让我选，我会喜欢这样的情节哦。

**柯莱:** 亲爱的砂糖，我虽然早已不是孩童，可就在短短的日子里，我似乎又长大了，变得顽强，可以支撑起什么。

**柯莱:** 神奇的你，会觉得这样的朋友很好玩吗？总是在生长，像植物一样蓬勃。

**柯莱:** 我来送别过去的自己，也知道这次之后她一定会离开。想到这点我甚至觉得孤单，陪伴我的懦弱居然也有离开的一天。

**柯莱:** 可心里某个地方，我又感到发自肺腑的喜悦。似乎很久很久之前，我就一直渴望迈出这一步，走向如今的自己。
